1b1('dul ba): @#//rgya gar skad du/brA ti mo k+Sha sU tra/bod skad du/so sor tha rang ba'i mdo//bam po dang po//thams cad mkhyen pa la phyag 'tshal lo//snyan 
1b2('dul ba): pa'i ba dan 'jig rten gsum du grags//dam pa'i chos sgra seng ge'i sgra bsgrags pa//thams cad mkhyen pa dkon mchog mdzod brnyed pa//zhabs la tshangs dbang 
1b3('dul ba): gtsug gi nor bus gtugs//sdug bsngal rgya mtsho ting mtha' med rgal ba//'gro ba'i gtso la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te//thams cad mkhyen pa'i bslab gzhi dkon mchog 
1b4('dul ba): snod//'phags pa'i tshogs kyi dbus su dbye bar bya//sangs rgyas 'dul ba gang chen mtsho//gting mtha' med pa thams cad kyi//gnas pa'i snying dang snying po ni//so sor thar 
1b5('dul ba): pa 'di yin no//'di ni dam chos rgyal po yi//chos kun gyi ni 'dren pa mchog/'di ni dge slong tshor tshogs kyi//bslab zong tshor khang chen po yin//tshul khrims 'chal bas zin 
1b6('dul ba): rnams kyi//gdug pa rnams sel sman 'di yin//'di ni lang tsho rnam 'khrul pa'i//rkang gnyis gzhon nu'i lcags kyu yin//mtsho bas zab pa'i 'khor ba las//sgrol ba'i rgal 
1b7('dul ba): zabs 'di yin te//'di ni bzang 'gror 'gro ba yi//des pa'i chu lon zam pa yin//'di ni nyon mongs pham byed lam//rgyal po yi ni 'dren pa mchog/'di na thar pa'i grong 
1b8('dul ba): 'jug pa'i//them skas gzhi dang 'dra bar gnas//nga ni mya ngan 'das gyur na//'di ni khyed kyi ston pa'o zhes//rang byung nyid kyis gus bcas par//nan tan dge slong 
2a1('dul ba): @#//tshogs 'dun bstod//sangs rgyas zhes bya'i sgra 'di yang//'jig rten dag na rab tu dkon//mi nyid rnyed pa shin tu dka'//rab tu 'byung ba shin du dkon//de bzhin rab tu byung rnams kyi//tshul khrims phun sum tshogs rab dkon//tshul khrims yongs su
2a2('dul ba): dag gyur kyang//grogs bzang shin du rnyed pa dka'//'jig rten sangs rgyas 'byung ba dang/mi dang rab tu 'byung ba dang//tshul khrims phun sum tsho gas pa dang/grogs bzang rnyed dka' rnyed gyur nas/mkhas pa bdag la legs 'dod cing//de dag 'bras bcas byad 'dod pa'i/sdom
2a3('dul ba): brtson rnams kyis so sor thar//mnyan pa'i phyir ni 'bad par bya//dge slong dbang dul kun dbang bo//sangs rgyas mi 'gyur rab byung ba//nges par thar pa 'dod rnams kyis//so sor thar pa rtag tu srungs//bskal pa bye ba rnams su yang//so sor thar pa thos pa dang//gzung dang 'dzin pa
2a4('dul ba): brnyed dka' ste//sgrub pa'ang shin tu rnyed par dka'//sangs rgyas rnams ni 'byung ba bde//chos bstan pa yang bde ba yin//dge 'dun 'thun pa bde ba ste//'thun pa rnams kyi dka' thub bde//'phags pa rnams ni mthong ba bde//dam pa dag dang 'grogs pa bde//byis pa rnams ni ma mthong
2a5('dul ba): na//rtag tu bde ba nyid du 'gyur//tshul khrims ldan pa mthong ba bde//mang du thos pa mthong ba bde//yang srid rnam par grol ba yi//dgra bcom bdag mthong ba bde//'jug dogs bde ba'i chu klung bde//chos la goms pa'i skya bo bde//shes rab thob par
2a6('dul ba): gyur pa bde/nga 'o nga rgyal zad pa bde//nges par byas shing dbang po thul ba dag/dgon pa zhi ba rnams su rgas gyur dang//mang du thos pa nags kyi nad dag tu//lang tsho yol ba rnams kyis gnas pa bde//tshe dang ldan bdag so ka rnams kyi 'das pa dang/lhag ma ji tsam pa de tsam//
2a7('dul ba): tshe dang ldan pa dag rga ba dang 'chi ba mngon par 'od zhing ston pa'i bstan pa yang 'jig par 'gyur bas/tshe dang ldan pa dag gis bag yod pas rnal 'byor du bya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi byang chub dang/gzhan yang gang dag de lta
2a8('dul ba): bu dang 'thun pa dge ba'i chos byang chub kyi phyogs rnams kyang bag yod pas 'thob po//bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos kyi dge 'dun ni don nyung ba dang bya ba nyung ba yin pas dge 'dun gyis thog mar bya ba ci yod//tshe dang ldan pa dag ma lhags pa rnams la 'dun pa dang/yongs su
2b1('dul ba): dag pa dris shig/dris nas kyang brjod par bya'o//shAkya seng dge de la ni/seng mo bcu yi thal sbyar te//so sor thar pa gdon par byas/'dul ba'i don du da las nyon//thos nas drang srong chen po yis//ji skad gsungs bzhin bsgrub bya zhing //kha na ma tho phra rnams la/rtun cing 
2b2('dul ba): byed pa nyid du gyis//rtag du 'bad pas 'da' ba yi//sems rta kha blan dka' ba la/'thun pa gzer rnon brgya ba yi//srab ni so sor thar 'di yin//tshe ba gang dag dag tsam gyis//ldog cing 'tshams las mi 'da' ba//de dag mi rta bzang po ste/nyon mongs g.yul las ngas 
2b3('dul ba): rgyal 'gyur//su la srab 'di med pa dang //nam du'ang 'dod par mi 'gyur ba//de dag nyon mongs g.yul gyis dkrugs//brjod bral rnam par 'khyam par 'gyur//dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol//deng dge 'dun gyis gso sbyong bcu bzhi pa 'am bcwa lnga pa ste/
2b4('dul ba): gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bgrod na dge 'dun gyis nang bar mdzod cig dang /deng dge 'dun gso sbyong mdzad de so sor thar pa'i mdo gdon pa gdon to//'di ni gsol ba'o//tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag cag gso sbyong bya ste/so sor thar pa'i mdo gdonapa gdon gyis/khyed cag 
2b5('dul ba): su la ltung ba yod pa des mthol cig/ltung ba med na cang mi smra shig/cang mi smra na bdag gis tshe dang ldan pa dag yongs su dag par rig par bya'o//'di ltar so sor dris nas dge slong gis lan btab pa de bzhin du dge slong gi 'khor 'di lta bur yang lan gsum gyi bar du skrag 
2b6('dul ba): par bya ba yin no//yang dge slong gang dge slong gi 'khor 'di ltar bur lan gsum gyi bar du bsgrags pa na ltung ba yod la dran bzhin mi mthol na de shes bzhin du brdzun du smra ba yin no//tshe dang ldan pa dag shes bzhin du brdzun du sma ba ni bcom ldan 'das kyis bar du gcod pa'i chos su 
2b7('dul ba): gsungs so//de bas na dge slong ltung ba byung ba rnam par dag par 'dod pas ltung ba yod dran bzhin mthong ba mthol bar bya'o//mthol na de bde ba la reg par gnas par 'gyur ro//ma mthol ma bshags na ni mi 'gyur ro//tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag gis so sor thar pa'i mdo gdon pa'i 
2b8('dul ba): gleng bzhi bton zin to//de la bdag gis tshe dang ldan pa dag la ci 'di la khyed yongs su dag gam zhes dri'o//ci 'di la khyed yongs su dang gam zhes lan gnyis lan gsum du dri'o//'di la tshe dang ldan pa dag yongs su dag na 'di ltar cang mi smra bas/de de bzhin du 'dzin 
3a1('dul ba): @#//
3b1('dul ba): de yang pham par gyur pa yin gyis gnas par mi bya'o//tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag gis pham par gyur pa'i chos bzhi po dag bton zin to//dge slong gis de dag las ltung ba gang yang rung ba zhig byas na thog ma ji lta bar phyis kyang de bzhin du pham par gyur pa yin te 
3b2('dul ba): dge slong rnams dang lhan cig gnas pa dang longs spyod du mi dbang gis gnas par mi bya'o//de la bdag gis tshe dang ldan pa dag la ci 'di la khyod yongs su dag gam zhes dri'o//ci 'di la khyed yongs su dag gam zhes lan gnyis lan gsum du dri'o//'di la 
3b3('dul ba): tshe dang ldan pa dag yongs su dag na 'di ltar cang mi smra bas de de bzhin du 'dzin to//sdom la/khu ba 'dzin pa 'khrig tshig bsnyen bkur smyan//khang pa khang chen dang ni gzhi med pa//bag tsam dge 'dun dbye na dang de rjes phyogs//khyim sun 'byin dang bka' blo mi bde 
3b4('dul ba): ba'o//tshe dang ldan pa dag dge 'dun lhag ma'i chos bcu gsum po 'di dag ni zla ba phyed phyed cing so sor thar pa'i mdo 'don pa las 'byung ngo //bsams bzhin du khu ba phyung na rmi lam gyi ma gtogs te dge 'dun lhag ma'o//yang dge slong gang dral cing gyur 
3b5('dul ba): pa'i sems kyis bud med kyi yul dang lhan cig lus reg par byed dam/lag pa nas bzung ngam/dpung pa nas bzung ngam/lan bu nas bzung ngam/yan lag dang nying lag gang yang rung ba la nom pa dang nyug pa bdag gir byed na dge 'dun lhag ma'o//yan dge 
3b6('dul ba): slong gang dral cing gyur pa'i sems kyis bud med kyis yul dang lhan cig gnas ngan len gyi tshig sdig pa can tshogs par mi dbyung ba 'khrig pa las byung ba dang ldan pa dag skyes bus na chung la ji lta ba bzhin du smras na dge 'dun lhag ma'o/yang dge slong gang 
3b7('dul ba): dral cing gyur pa'i sems gyis bud med kyi lus kyi mdun du bdag nyid kyi lus kyi bsnyen bkur bya ba'i phyir 'di lta ste/de lta bu'i dge slong tshul khrims dang ldan pa/dge ba'i chos can/tshangs par spyod pa la 'di ltar 'khrig pa las byung ba dang/ldan pa'i chos 'dis 
3b8('dul ba): bsnyen bkur byas na//su 'di ni bsnyen bkur byas pa rnams kyi nang na mchog yin no zhes bsngags pa brjod na dge 'dun lhag ma'o////yang dge slong gang bud med la skyes pa'i tshig dang /skyes pa la bud med kyi tshig gis chung ma nyid dam/mdza' na mo nyid du smyan byed na tha 
4a1('dul ba): @#//na thang 'ga' phrad pa la yang rung ste dge slong 'dun lhag ma'o//dge slong gis bdag gis bslangs pa bdag po med pa/bdag gis phyir khang pa rtsig tu 'jug na dge slong des khang pa tshad bzhin du rtsig tu chug cig/de la khang pa'i tshad ni 'di yin te 
4a2('dul ba): nang gi srid du bde bar gshegs pa'i mtho'i mtho bcu gnyis/zheng du mtho bdun no//gzhi ba lta ba'i phyir dge slong des dge slong dag dkri bar bya'o//khrid pa'i dge slong dag gis kyang gzhi rung ba dang /rtsod pa med pa dang /rtsam du rung bar blta bar bya'o//gal te dge slong gi gzhi 
4a3('dul ba): mi rung ba'am/rtsod pa dang bcas pa'am/brtsam du mi rung bar bdag gis bslangs pa bag po med pa bdag gi phyir khang pa rtsig du 'jug gam/gzhi blta ba'i phyir dge slong dag kyang mi khrid dam/dge slong khrid pa dag la gzhi mi ston tam/tshad las 'das na 
4a4('dul ba): dge 'dun lhag ma'o//dge slon gis bdag po yod pa dge 'dun gyi phyir gtsug lag khang chen po rtsig tu 'jug na gzhi blta ba'i phyir dge slon des dge slong dag bkri bar bya'o//khrid pa'i bde gshegs dag gis kyang gzhi rung pa dang /rtsod pa med pa ma/brtsam du rung 
4a5('dul ba): bar blta bar bya'o//gal de dge slong gis gzhi mi rung ba'am/rtsod pa dag bcas pa'am/rtsam du mi rung bar bdag po yod pa dge 'dug gyi phyar gtsug lag khang chen po'i rtsig tu 'jug gam/gzhi blta ba 'i phyir dge slong dag kyang ma khrid dam/dge slong khrid ba dag 
4a6('dul ba): la gzhi mi ston na dge 'dun lhag ma'o//yang dge slong gang khros shing zhe sdang bar gyur nas/ci nas kyang 'di tshangs par spyod pa dang dbral lo snyam nas/dge slong dag pa ltung ba med pa la/gzhi med par pham par gyur pa'i chos kyis skur ba las de dus 
4a7('dul ba): gzhan zhig na dris kyang rung /ma dris kyang rung /rtsod pa de yang gzhi med pa yin la/dge slong yang zhe sdang la gnas pas zhe sdang gis smras so zhe na dge 'dun lhag ma'o/yang dge slong gang khros shig zhe sdang bar gyur nas ci nas kyang 'di tshangs bar spyod pa dang 
4a8('dul ba): dbral lo snyam nas/dge blong dag pa ltung ba med ba la gzhan gyi cha ma yin pa dang 'thun pa pham par gyur pa'i chos gyis skur ba las/de dus gzhan zhig na dris kyang run/ma dris kyang rung /rtso ba de yang gzhan gyi cha ma yin pa 'thun pa yin la/rtsod 
4b1('dul ba): pa de gzhan gyi cha ma yin pa dang 'thun pas bag tsam las bsams pa tsam gyi chos 'ga' zhig blangs par gyur la/dge slong yang zhe sdang la gnas pas zhe sdang gis smras so zhe na dge 'dun lhag ma'o//yang dge slong gang dge 'dun 'thun pa dbye ba'i phyir rtul 
4b2('dul ba): bar byed cing dbyen byed par 'gyur pa'i rtsod pa yang dag par blangs nas rab tu bzung ste 'dug na/dge slong de la dge slong rnams kyis 'di skad ces/tshe dang ldan pa khyod dge 'dun 'thun pa de bye ba'i phyir rtul bar ma byed cig/dbyen byed par 'gyur 
4b3('dul ba): ba'i rtsod pa yang dag par blags nas rab tu bzung ste ma 'dug cig/tshe dang ldan pa dge 'dun dang lhan cig 'thun par gyis shig/dge 'dun 'thun mi phyed/kun tu dga' mi rtsod//mchog gcig 'don pa gcig cing chu dang 'o ma 'dres pa lta bur gyur la 
4b4('dul ba): ston pa'i bstan pa yang gsal bar byed na bde ba la reg par gnas par 'gyur gyis/tshe dang ldan pa khyod de 'dun 'byed par byed pa'i gzhi 'di lta bu 'di thong shig ces bsgo bar bya'o//dge slong de la dge slong rna mas kyis de skad bsgo ba na/gal te gzhi de gtod na 
4b5('dul ba): de lta na legs/gal te mi gtong na gzhi te gtong bar bya ba'i phyir lan gnyis lan gsum du yang dag par bsgo bar bya'o//yang dag par bstan par bya'o//lan gnyis lan gsum du yag dag par sgo/yang dag par bstan pa na gzhi de gtod na de lta na legs/
4b6('dul ba): gal te mi gtong na dge 'dun lhag ma'o//dge slong de'i grogs byed pa'i dge slong mi mthun par smra ba nyid kyi rjes su phyogs pa gcig gam gnyis sam mang po dag yod cing /gal te de dag dge slong rnams la 'di skad ces/tshe dang ldan pa dag khyed cag dge 
4b7('dul ba): yang rung //sdig kyang rung /dge slong 'di la ci yang ma smra shig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa dag dge slong 'di ni chos smra ba/'dul ba smra ba/dge slong 'di na chos dang 'dul ba yang dag par blangs nas rab tu bzung ste rjes su tha snyad 'dogs par byed pa/
4b8('dul ba): dge slong 'di ni shes bzhin du smra'i mi shes par ma yin pa'i phyir te/dge slong 'di gang la 'dod pa'i cing bzod pa de la bdag cag kyang 'dod cing bzod do zhes zer na/dge slong de dag la dge slong rnams kyis 'di skad ces/tshe dang lda na pa dge byed cag de skad ces tshe

5b1('dul ba): sam/de dag sdig pa kun du spyod pa mthong ngam/thos sam/rab tu shes na dge slong de dag la dge slong rnams kyis 'di skad ces/tshe dang ldan pa dag khyim sun 'byin pa sdig pa'i chos kun du spyod pa dag yin te/khyid gyis khyim dag sun phyung bar yang mthong zhing thos 
5b2('dul ba): la rab tu shes khyed sdig pa kun tu spyod par yang mthong zhing thos la rab tu shes kyis/tshe dang ldan pa dag khyed 'di na gnas pas chog gis gnas 'di nas deng shig ces bsgo bar bya'o//gal te de dag dge slong rnams la 'di skad ces/tshe dang lda pa dag 'di ni 
5b3('dul ba): dge slong kha cig 'dun pas 'gro ba/zhe sdang gis 'gro ba/gtig mug gis 'gro ba/'jigs pas 'gro ba dag yin te/'di ltar ltung ba 'gro ba kho na las dge slong kha cig ni skrod par byed/kha cig ni skrod bar mi byed do//zhes zer na/dge slong de dag la dge

5b6('dul ba): sun 'byin pa sdig pa'i chos kun du spyod pa dag yin te/khyed nyid kyis khyim dag sun phyung bar mthong zhing thos la rab tu shes/khyed sdig pa kun du spyod par yang mthong zhing thos la rab du shes pa'i phyir te/tshe dang ldan pa dag 'dun pas 'gro ba zhes bya ba'i tshig gi

5b8('dul ba): gtong na gzhi de gtong bar bya ba'i phyir lan gnyis lan gsum du yang dag par bsgo bar bya/yang dag par bstan par bya'o//lan gnyis lan gsum du yag dag par bsgo/yang dag par bstan pa na gzhi de gtong na de lta na log gal te mi gtong na dge 'dun lhag ma'o//

6b2('dul ba): nas de'i 'og tu zhag drug gi bar du dge 'dun mgu bar bya ba spyad par bya'o//dge slong gis mgu par bya ba spyad nas/dbyung bar bya ba la thogs te chos dang 'thun par byas la/dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi sems mgu par byas na gang na dge slong nyi shu'i tshogs kyi 
6b3('dul ba): dge 'dun yod pa der dge slong de dbyung bar bya'o//gal te dge slong nyi shur gcig gis ma tshang ba'i tshogs kyi dge 'dun gyis dge slong de 'byin par byed na/dge slong de yang ma phyin la dge slong de dag kyang smad par bya ba yin te/de la de ni cho ga yin no//de la bdag gis tshe 
6b4('dul ba): dang ldan pa dag la ci 'di la khyed yongs su dag gam zhes dri'o//ci 'di la khyed yongs su dang gam zhes lan gnyis lan gsum du dri'o//'di la tshe dang ldan pa dag yongs su dag na 'di ltar cang mi smra bas de bzhin du 'dzin to//sdom la/'dod byar dung dang mi rung ba'i/dben bskyabs

6b6('dul ba): la 'dug par byed cing /de la gal te dge bsnyen ma yid ches pa'i tshig dang ldan pas chos gsum po pham par 'gyur ba'am/dge 'dun lhag ma'am/ltung byed las chos gang yang rung bas smras par gyur la/dge slong gis 'dug par khas blangs na chos gsum 
6b7('dul ba): po pham par gyur pa'am/dge 'dun lhag ma'ang ma ltung byed las chos gang yar rung bas byed du gzhug cing dge slong de la dge bsnyen ma yid ches ba'i tshig dang ldan pas chos gong dang gang gis smras par gyur pa'i chos de dang des dge slong de byed du gzhug ste/de ni ma nges pa'i chos so//yang dge 
6b8('dul ba): slong gang bud med kyi yul la lhan cig gcig bu gcig dang dben pa skyab yod pa na 'dod pa byar mi rung bar bstan la 'dug par byed cing /de la gal te dge bsnyen pa yid ches pa'i tshig dang dang ldan pas chos gnyis po dge 'dun lhag ma'am/ltur byed las chos gang yang 
7a1('dul ba): @#//
7b1('dul ba): khyim bdag gi chung ma nye du ma yin pa'i gan du song ste/gos slong na dus ma gtogs te spang ba'i ltung byed do//de la dus ni 'di yin te/dge slong gos phrogs sam/gos rlag gam/gos tshig gam/gos rlung gis khyer ram/gos chus khyer na de la don dus yin no//
7b2('dul ba): dge slong gos phrogs sam /gos rlags gam/gos tshig gam/gos/rlung gis khyer ram/gos/chus khyer na khyim bdag gam khyim bdag gi chung ma nyed du ma yin pa'i gan du sogs la gos bslang bar bya'o//de la gal te bram ze 'am/khyim bdag dad pa can zhig gis 
7b3('dul ba): dus kyis gos mang po dag stobs la 'dod na dge slong des de las gos stong g.yogs smad g.yogs dad bcas pa'i mthar thug par blangs par bya'o//de las lhag par yin na spang ba'i ltung byed do/dge slong gi phyir khyim bdag gam/khyim bdag gis 
7b4('dul ba): chung ma nye du ma yin pas gos kyi rin dag spags nas bdag gis gos kyi rin 'di dag gis gos 'di dang 'di lta bu zhig nyos la dge slong ming 'di zhes bya ba 'ongs na/de la gos rung ba dus su bskon no snyam pa las/de la gal te dge slong de la sngar ma bstabs 
7b5('dul ba): par rnam par rtog pa 'ga' zhig byung ste/bzang po 'dod pa'i phyir khyim bdag gam/khyam bdag gis chung ma nye du ma yin pa de'i gan du song nas 'di skad ces/tshe dang ldan pas bdag gi phyir gos kyi rin gang dag spags pa'i gos kyi rin de dag gis tshe dang ldan pa 
7b6('dul ba): gos 'di dang 'di lta bu cig nyos la bdag la gos rung ba dus su legs par skon cig ces smras te/gos grub na spang ba'i ltung byed de/dge slong gi phyir khyim pa dag dang khyim bdag gi chung ma nye du ma yin pas bos kyi rin so so de dag spags nas/bdag cag gnyis 
7b7('dul ba): kyis gos kyi rin so so ba 'di dag gis gos 'di dag 'di lta bu so so ba dag nyos la/dge slong ming 'di zhes bya pa 'ongs pa dang /bdag cag gnyis kyis so so nas de la gos rung ba dus su bskon no snyam pa las/de na gal te dge slong de la sngar ma bstabs par 
7b8('dul ba): rnam par rtog pa 'ga' zhig byung ste/bzang po 'dod pa'i phyir khyim bdag dang /khyim bdag gi chu ma nye du ma yin pa de dag gi gan du song nas 'di skad ces/tshe dang ldan pa dag gis bdag gi phyir gos kyi rin so so ba gang dag spags pa'i gos kyi rin de dag

18b8('dul ba): 'gyur zhing //bzlog par gyur na zhi ba thob par 'gyur//sangs rgyas mi bzhugs 'jig rten tshigs bcad dang //mdo sde'i yan lag de dang de dag spyod//bslab 'jug med na 'dul ba'ang med 'gyur bas//de phyir rgyal ba dag pas chos 'di dkon//'jog bal sems 
19a1('dul ba): @#//glang lcags kyo 'di yin te//gdul bya ma dul ba yis srab 'di yin//ra ba'i mtshams kyi 'obs dang 'dra ba ste//zag pa kun gyi chu lon 'dul ba yin//rgyal ba ston pa de yi bstan bcos dag/mdo dang chos mngon yin gsungs 'dul ba ni//ston dang 
19a2('dul ba): bstan gcos dngos yin de yi phyir//gnyis gyur phyag bya sangs rgyas chos gcig gzhan//gang la brten te gnas pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//de la gus bcas rje sar bcas gyur cing //de ni mgon pos mgon yin skyabs gnas yin//de phyir rab bsgrims 'dul la phyag 
19a3('dul ba): 'tshal cig/gang zhig ston pa thugs rje'i bstan pa la//yang bar sems shing cung zad 'da' byed pa//de ni de las sdug bsngal gzhan dbang 'thob//smig tshal bregs pas a mra'i tshal nyams bzhin//'di na la las rgyal po'i tshig chen yang //bzlas na lan 'ga' 
19a4('dul ba): chad pa 'thob mi 'gyur//thub pa'i dka' lung tshul min bzla byas na//dud 'gror 'gro 'gyur e la'i mdab klu bzhin//so sor thar pa gang yin pa//sangs rgyas 'dul ba gang chen mtsho//gting mtha' med pa thams cad kyi//gnas pa'i snying dang snying po yin//'di ni 
19a5('dul ba): dam chos rgyal po yi//chos rnams kun gyi 'dren pa mchog/'di ni dge slong tshong tshogs kyi//bslab zong tshong khang chen po yin//tshul khrims 'chal bas zin rnams kyi//gdug pa rnam sel sman 'di yin//'di ni lang tsho rnam 'khrul ba'i//rkang gnyis gzhon 
19a6('dul ba): nu'i lcags kyu yin//mtsho bas zab pa'i 'khor ba las//sgrol ba'i rgal thabs 'di yin te//'di ni bzang 'gror 'gro ba yi//nges pa'i chu lon zam pa yin//'di ni nyon mongs pham byed lam//rgyal po yis ni 'dren pa mchog/'di ni thar pa'i grong 'jug 
19a7('dul ba): pa'i//them skas gzhi dang 'dra bar gnas//de ni mya ngan 'das gyur na//'di ni khyed kyi ston pa'o zhes//rang byung nyid kyis gus bcas pad nan tan dge slong tshogs mdun bstod//'dul 'di so sor thar pa de'i//rnam 'byed yin par dgongs pas 
19a8('dul ba): na//thar 'dod rnams kyis gus byas te//'di ni nan tan mnyan par bya//'dul ba la bstod pa rdzogs s+ho//bskal pa bye ba rnams su yang //'dul ba thos par 'gyur ba dang //gzung dang 'dzin pa rnyed dka' ste//sgrub pa'ang shin tu rnyed par dka'//sangs rgyas 
19b1('dul ba): rnams ni 'byung ba bde//chos bstan pa yang bde ba yin//dge 'dun 'thun pa bde ba ste//'thun pa rnams kyi bka' thub pa de//'phags pa rnams ni mthong ba bde//dam pa dag dang 'grogs pa bde//byis pa rnams ni ma mthong na//rtag tu bde ba nyid du 'gyur//tshul 
19b2('dul ba): khrims ldan pa mthong ba bde//mang du thos pa mthong ba bde//yang srid rnam par grol ba yi//dgra bcom pa dag mthong ba bde//'jug ngogs bde ba'i chu klung bde//chos la goms pa'i skye bo bde//shes rab thob par gyur pa bde//nga 'o nga rgyal zad pa bde//nges par byas 
19b3('dul ba): shing dbang po thul ba dag/dgon pa zhi ba rnams su gas gyur dang //mang du thos pa nags kyi nang dag tu//lang tsho yol ba rnams kyis gnas pa bde//shAkya seng dge de la ni//sor mo bcu yi thal sbyar te//'dul ba rab tu gdon par byas//gdul ba'i don du nga la nyon//
19b4('dul ba): thos nas drang srong chen po yis//ji skad gsungs bzhin bsgrub bya zhing //kha na ma tho phra rnams la//rdzun cing byed pa nyid du gyis//rtag tu 'bad pas 'da' ba yi//sems rta kha bla na dka' ba la//'thun pa gzer rnon brgya pa yi//srib ni 'dul ba 'di yin no//che ba gang 
19b5('dul ba): dag dag tsam gyis//ldog cing mtshams las mi 'da' ba//de dag mi rta bzang po ste//nyon mongs g.yul las nges rgal 'gyur//su la srab 'di med pa dang //nam du'ang 'dod par mi 'gyur ba//de dag nyon mongs g.yul gyis dkrugs//'jog bral rnam par 'khyam 
19b6('dul ba): par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos kyi dge 'dun lo bcu gnyis su skyon med cing chu bur med la/bcom ldan 'das kyis nyan thos rnams kyi so sor thar pa'i mdo gdon pa yang mdor bsdus te/sdig pa thams cad mi bya ste//dge ba phun sum tshogs par 
19b7('dul ba): bya//rang gis sems ni yongs su gdul//'di ni sangs rgyas bstan pa yin//lus kyi sdom pa legs pa ste//ngag gi sdom pa'ang legs pa yin//yid kyi sdom pa'ang legs pa ste//thams cad du ni sdom pa legs//kun du bsdams pa'i dge slong ni//sdug 
19b8('dul ba): bsngal kun las rab tu 'grol//ngag rnams bsrung zhing yid kyis rab rab bsdams te//lus kyis mi dge ba dag mi byed cing //las lam gsum po 'di dag rab sbyangs na//drang srong gsungs pa'i lam ni thob par 'gyur//zhes bton par gyur to//lo bcu 
20a1('dul ba): @#//gsum la bab pa na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das yul spong byed dag na ka lan da ka'i grong na bzhugs so/de'i tshe na ka lan dka'i grong na ka lan dka'i bu bzang byin zhes bya ba phyug po/nor mang ba longs spyod che ba/yongs su gzung ba yangs shing rgya che ba rnam thos kyi bu'i 
20a2('dul ba): nor dang ldan pa/rnam thos kyi bu la nor gyis 'gran pa zhig 'dug ste/des rigs mnyam pa las chung ma blangs nas de de dang lhan cig rtse bar byed dga' bar byed/dga' mgur spyod par byed do//de dus gzhan zhig na sangs rgyas la mngon par dang /chos dang dge 'dun la 
20a3('dul ba): mngon par dang /sangs rgyas la skyabs su song /chos dang dge 'dun skyabs su song zhing /des srog gcod pa spangs te/srog gcod pa las phyir log ma byin par len pa dang /'dod pa la log par g.yem pa dang /brdzun du smra ba dang /'bru'i chang dang /bcos pa'i chang myos 
20a4('dul ba): bar 'gyur ba bag med pa'i gnas spangs te/'bru'i chang dang /btsos pa'i chang myos bar 'gyur ba bag med pa'iganas las phyir log go/de dad pa snga ma de nyid rjes su spel zhing skra dang kha spu bregs te gos ngur smrig dag bgos nas yang dag pa kho nar dad pas

20a6('dul ba): ba la rab tu byung nas 'di lta te/sngon khyim pa bzhin du nye du rnams dngalhan cig 'dre zhing gnas pa na bdag gis ma rnyed de rnyed pa ma yin zhing nyes pa rnyed de legs pa rnyed pa ma yin gyis/bdag nye du rnams dang 'bral ba'i phyir ltung bzed dang chos gos thogs te 
20a7('dul ba): ldongs rgyur 'gro'o snyam mo//de nas ka lan dka'i phu tshe dang ldan pa bzang byi na nye du rnams dang 'bral pa'i phyir ltung bzed dang tshos gos thogs te ljongs rgyur song ngo //de'i tshe na mu ge nyam nga ba dgon pa slongs mo pas zas rnyed par dka' zhing pha ma dag kyang rang gi bu dag 
20a8('dul ba): la zas sbyin pa dang bya bar sems pa dka' na slongs mos pa dag lta la smos kyang ci dgos pa byung ngo //de nas ka lan dka'i bu tshe dang ldan pa bzang byin 'di snyam du sems te/de ltar mu ge nyam nga ba dgon pa'i slongs mo pas zas rnyed bar dka' zhing pha ma dag gyang 
20b1('dul ba): rang gi bu ngag la zas sbyin pa dang bya bar sems pa dka' na slongs mo pa dag lta la smos kyang ci dgos pa byung la/bdag la nye du longs spyod chen po dang ldan pa dag yod pas bdag ka lar dka'i grong du song ste/nye du rnams kyi gan du song la dge 'dun la zas dag 
20b2('dul ba): sbyor du gzhug cing dge 'dun la skyo ma dag dang /thug pa'i gtung ba dag dang /rtag res 'khor dag dang /'gron du bos pa dag dang /brgyad ston dang /bcu bzhi ston dang /nya ston dang /spyan gzigs phran tshegs phran tshegs sbyor du gzhug go snyam mo//de nas ka lan dka'i bu tshe 
20b3('dul ba): dang ldan pa bzang byin ljongs su ci dgar 'dug nas lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs te ka lan dka'i grong gal ba der rgyu zhing song ba na mthar gyis rgyu ba'i tshe ka lan dka'i grong du phyin nas ka lan dka'i grong na dgon pa'i spyil bu na gnas so//de nas ka lan dka'i
20b4('dul ba): tshe dang ldan pa bzang byin nye du rnams kyi gan du songaste phyin nas de dag gi mdun du sangs rgyas dang /chos dang /dge 'dun gyi sngags pa brjod de/dge 'dun la zas dge sbyor du 'dzud cing /dge 'dun la skyo ma dag dag dang /thug pa'i btung ba dag dang /rtag nges 'khor dag

20b6('dul ba): kyis rgyu bar gyur to//de dus gzhan zhig na dang po kho nar dge slong gi dge 'dun thug pa bsod pas tshim par phyes nas snga dro sham thabs bgos te lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs nas ka lan dka'i grong du bsod snyoms la zhugs pa na mthar gyis ka lan dka'i grong du gsong

21a2('dul ba): bu tshe dang ldan pa bzang byin gyi ma 'di snyam du sems te/kye ma ma la ka lan da ka'i bu bzang byin 'dod pa 'am/mngon par mi dga' ba 'am/rnam par 'khyam 'dod par gyur cing dge sbyong gi tshul don du mi gnyer ba 'am/dge sbyong gi tshul gyis sgyid lug pa'am

22a8('dul ba): non cing de 'dod chags kyis sems zim gyis non pas yongs su gdung ba'i lus dang /yongs su gdung ba'i sems dang ldan pa zhing /de yongs su gdung bzhin pa'i lus dang /yongs su gsung gzhin pa'i sems kyis mal 'di skad ces smras so/yum
